# Same-game audit

Generated by `node scripts/same-game-audit.mjs` over 42 contracts in every epoch bundle. The harness cross-checks those JSON bundles against `ContractFamilies.listContracts()`, then reads live seam positions and the buildable, tape-action, standing-order, hero resume-key, upgrade, research, and XP registries from source.

## Summary

- agent-exceeds: 0
- agent-lacks: 511
- equal: 1252
- not-offered: 0
- divergence rows: 511
- total measured rows: 1763

## New divergence classes beyond the seed

- **Contract reachability:** 4 browser-offered contracts are rejected by the headless door; 0 contracts are refused by both species and sit outside the same-game universe.
- **Browser-menu versus door:** 0 independent buildable rows disagree; 24 rows are downstream of 4 contract-admission refusals today.
- **Build orientation:** human `place_build` tapes and standing-order `BUILD` both carry `rotationSteps` 0..3; omission defaults to 0.
- **Additional tape-only actions:** `restart`, `debug_spawn`, `debug_xp`, `set_agent_rung`, `set_agent_ability` have no matching standing-order verb or cited exemption.

### Class-8 cited exemptions

| tape action | reason | citation |
|---|---|---|
| death_action | Post-death overlay transition; the headless terminal is already the run end. | src/game/Game.ts:2902 |
| research_pick / research_skip | Between-run science progression lives outside the run window. | src/game/Game.ts:2909 |
| set_pause | Pacing only; the agent door is turn-based. | src/game/Game.ts:2886 |
| skip_ceremony | Baron ceremony is presentation-only; headless spawns the Baron directly without a ceremony gate. | src/sim/HeadlessContractSim.ts:1394 |

## Worst offenders

The widest gates in the county are `e6-showroom` (33), `e9-old-canal` (33), `e9-seed-run` (33), `e10-river` (32), `e1-baron` (10). Buildables now share one manifest rulebook; the remaining debt is contract reachability plus human choices and abilities without a standing-order trail.

### Re-aimed guard teeth proof

Manufactured defect: added `phantom-contract` inside the guarded `door-contracts` fence. RED: `AssertionError [ERR_ASSERTION]: Expected values to be strictly deep-equal`, with `+ phantom-contract` under `skill.md door-contracts match SUPPORTED_CONTRACTS in HeadlessContractSim`. The edit was reverted byte-identically (`sha256 d3b1b7c0a4a899c2ad82b138c9a67cae7598b34ebb0e7fae7aef11049121b15b`) and the same focused guard returned 1/1 green.

## Controls parity (ADR-005)

ADR-005 (owner, 2026-09-07): the agent controls exactly what a human controls.

Every row above measures a MECHANIC and is blind to which BODY reaches it, so a mechanic both species reach through different bodies reads `equal` here forever. This section asks the owner's question instead: for each door verb, which body it acts from in GR-SIM, and whether a human at a plain boot has a control that reaches it. Nothing behind `?debug` counts as a human control (the whole `__GR_TEST__` bridge is installed only under that flag, src/game/Game.ts:2221), and the solo browser door refuses `MOVE_HERO` outright (src/game/Game.ts:2734).

- equal: 15
- agent-only: 0
- human-only-richer: 0
- verbs measured: 15

The map is hand-maintained and its key set is checked against the live `StandingOrder` union: a verb added to the door without a row here throws before any row is emitted, so the door cannot grow a power without someone answering "and what does the human press for this?".

**F-RPA-1: closed.** The older `quoted()` verb registry now sees every declared verb.

### The human's Prospector policy surface

| policy | what it changes | where the human sets it | door verb | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| trust rung L0..L3 (granted / revoked) | which abilities the Prospector may use at all; the rung a rider order must clear | src/ui/ProspectorPanel.ts:116 | none | human-only-richer |
| ability grants (auto_collect, auto_repair, light_duty, auto_pan, place_building) | which chores the Prospector performs unattended, per ability | src/ui/ProspectorPanel.ts:152 | none | human-only-richer |
| repair under N% HP (0..100, default 60) | the damage threshold below which the Prospector tends a work | src/ui/ProspectorPanel.ts:164 | REPAIR_UNDER | equal |
| act after N seconds idle (0..60, default 0.8) | how long the Prospector must be idle before an unattended chore starts | src/ui/ProspectorPanel.ts:167 | none | human-only-richer |
| dispatch to a named seam or sluice | sends the Prospector to ONE published work target and pans it there | src/ui/ProspectorDispatchInput.ts:61 | HARVEST | equal |
| walk the hero (the Prospector drifts to it) | the ONLY positional control a human has over the Prospector: with no work assigned it walks back to the hero every step, so where the hero stands is where the Prospector ends up | src/agent/Embodiment.ts:287 | MOVE_HERO | equal |
